Verifier coverage · Task brief

Cascadia Founders Summit - Executive portrait set

A cell names the verifier for that rule and file. “n/a” means the rule does not apply. “Not scored” means it applies but the requested finished file lacks the evidence needed to verify it. It is not counted as covered. Hover for the reason; full details are also listed below.

Score only the listed current Auto and Human verifiers. A design target marked "Not scored" in coverage remains a client instruction but is excluded from the score denominator: the requested delivery does not provide enough evidence to verify it. Do not infer a pass or failure. If a listed measurement cannot be completed, leave it unanswered and record the missing evidence; do not guess. Human answers remain Yes/No. Historical run results are not current scores.

These are not verifiers and are excluded from the score denominator.

  • Display / H1 announcement headline size: 96 px.
    Not scored: The exact 96 px headline size cannot be measured reliably in a delivered PNG; the brand value is a design target. Rule T01b checks that the headline is the largest text.

    Not scored: The exact 104 px leading cannot be measured reliably in a delivered PNG; the brand value is a design target, and it applies only if the headline wraps to two lines.

    Not scored: The exact -10 headline tracking cannot be measured reliably in a delivered PNG; the brand value is a design target.
